    37	
    38	def saved_quotes_limit_for_plan(plan: Optional[str]) -> int:
    39	    """Same dict-lookup convention as documents.py's FREE/PLUS/PRO_MAX_DOCUMENTS
    40	    enforcement (numeric sentinel for "unlimited", not the boolean-gate
    41	    convention layout translation uses)."""
    42	    normalized = (plan or "free").lower()
    43	    getter = _LIMIT_BY_PLAN.get(normalized, _LIMIT_BY_PLAN["free"])
    44	    return int(getter())
    45	
    46	
    47	def compute_quote_hash(quote_text: str, page: int, page_end: int) -> str:
    48	    """SERVER-side dedup/idempotency key (§8.1's dedup-key philosophy,
    49	    scoped to one (user, document) pair via the table's UNIQUE constraint
    50	    rather than needing document_id baked into the hash itself):
    51	    normalized quote text + the VERIFIED page range — never the caller's
    52	    raw text or an unverified page guess. Callers must pass the page/
    53	    page_end that came back from verify_saved_quote()/quote_search(), not
    54	    anything client-supplied, or dedup becomes forgeable (two different
    55	    users' honest saves of the same passage should collide; a client
    56	    claiming a fake page range to dodge the unique constraint must not)."""
    57	    normalized_text, _ = normalize(quote_text or "", fuzzy=True)
    58	    payload = f"{normalized_text}|{page}|{page_end}"
    59	    return hashlib.sha256(payload.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    60	
    61	
    62	async def count_active_saved_quotes(db: AsyncSession, user_id: uuid.UUID) -> int:
    63	    """§8.4 point 2: ACTIVE = currently-existing rows, counted ACROSS all of
    64	    the user's documents. There is no soft-delete flag — a DELETE is what
    65	    "frees a slot" means, so a plain COUNT(*) is always the current truth."""
    66	    result = await db.execute(
    67	        sa.select(sa.func.count()).select_from(SavedQuote).where(SavedQuote.user_id == user_id)
    68	    )
    69	    return int(result.scalar() or 0)

    72	async def get_existing_saved_quote(
    73	    db: AsyncSession, *, user_id: uuid.UUID, document_id: uuid.UUID, quote_hash: str,
    74	) -> Optional[SavedQuote]:

    76	        sa.select(SavedQuote)
    77	        .where(SavedQuote.user_id == user_id)
    78	        .where(SavedQuote.document_id == document_id)
    79	        .where(SavedQuote.quote_hash == quote_hash)
    80	    )
    81	    return result.scalar_one_or_none()

    84	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    85	class SaveQuoteOutcome:
    86	    row: Optional[SavedQuote]  # None only when limit_reached
    87	    created: bool
    88	    limit_reached: bool
    89	    active_count: int  # meaningful on the limit_reached path; 0 on an idempotent hit
    90	
    91	
    92	async def save_quote(
    93	    db: AsyncSession, *, user: User, document: Document, card: QuoteCard,
    94	) -> SaveQuoteOutcome:
    95	    """Idempotent, cap-aware insert.
    96	
    97	    `card` MUST already be the output of a server-side re-verification
    98	    (quote_search_service.verify_saved_quote() or quote_search()'s own
    99	    cards) — every persisted trust column is copied from it as-is, never
   100	    recomputed here and never sourced from raw request data.
   101	
   102	    FIX-1 (Codex M3 r1 HIGH — cap race): the idempotency check AND the cap
   103	    check (count_active_saved_quotes vs saved_quotes_limit_for_plan) now
   104	    run INSIDE the same serialized critical section as the insert — Codex's
   105	    exact finding was that the old code ran the count check in the API
   106	    layer, OUTSIDE any lock and outside this function entirely: at
   107	    limit-1 rows, two concurrent DISTINCT saves (different quote_hash) both
   108	    read "count < limit" and both commit, overshooting the cap (reproduced
   109	    directly: 3 concurrent saves at 29/30 all succeeded, final count 32).
   110	
   111	    Serialized via a Postgres TRANSACTION-SCOPED advisory lock keyed by the
   112	    user's id (`pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(user_id))`) — acquired
   113	    FIRST, before either check. A second concurrent call for the SAME user
   114	    blocks on this statement until the first call's transaction ends
   115	    (commit or rollback, which releases the lock automatically), at which
   116	    point its own count query sees a fresh READ COMMITTED snapshot
   117	    including whatever the first call just committed. Chosen over
   118	    SELECT...FOR UPDATE on the `users` row (this codebase's existing
   119	    reconcile_credits() precedent) specifically to AVOID taking a real row
   120	    lock on `users`: that would also serialize against unrelated
   121	    concurrent work touching the same row (credit-balance debits from
   122	    chat/quote-search billing), coupling this feature's cap enforcement to
   123	    billing latency for no reason. The advisory lock only ever contends
   124	    with other save_quote() calls for the same user.
   125	
   126	    CRITICAL: every return path below explicitly commits (the lock is
   127	    released when ITS transaction ends, so leaving one open would hold the
   128	    lock — and block every other concurrent save for this user — for the
   129	    rest of the request). Deliberately commits rather than rolls back even
   130	    on a no-write decision (idempotent hit, cap reached): `expire_on_commit
   131	    =False` (app/models/database.py) means a commit does NOT expire
   132	    `document` or any row already loaded in this session, so the caller's
   133	    later SYNCHRONOUS reads (response serialization) stay safe — a
   134	    rollback here would expire those objects and reintroduce the exact
   135	    MissingGreenlet class already fixed for the PATCH endpoint.
   136	
   137	    SELECT-then-INSERT + IntegrityError-retry (same race biblio_service's
   138	    FIX-9 precedent handles, for a genuinely simultaneous identical save)
   139	    retries on a FRESH AsyncSessionLocal() session, not the just-rolled-
   140	    back `db` — see the prior real-concurrency finding in this function's
   141	    git history for why (NullPool-specific async/greenlet interaction
   142	    under true concurrent load; a production QueuePool-backed engine does
   143	    not exhibit it).
   144	    """
   145	    await db.execute(
   146	        sa.text("SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(:user_id))"),
   147	        {"user_id": str(user.id)},
   148	    )
   149	
   150	    quote_hash = compute_quote_hash(card.display_text, card.page, card.page_end)
   151	    existing = await get_existing_saved_quote(
   152	        db, user_id=user.id, document_id=document.id, quote_hash=quote_hash
   153	    )
   154	    if existing is not None:
   155	        await db.commit()  # release the lock; nothing written, see docstring
   156	        return SaveQuoteOutcome(row=existing, created=False, limit_reached=False, active_count=0)
   157	
   158	    active_count = await count_active_saved_quotes(db, user.id)
   159	    limit = saved_quotes_limit_for_plan(user.plan)
   160	    if active_count >= limit:
   161	        await db.commit()  # release the lock; nothing written, see docstring
   162	        return SaveQuoteOutcome(row=None, created=False, limit_reached=True, active_count=active_count)
   163	
   164	    row = SavedQuote(
   165	        user_id=user.id,
   166	        document_id=document.id,
   167	        page=card.page,
   168	        page_end=card.page_end,
   169	        quote_text=card.display_text,
   170	        bboxes=card.bboxes,
   171	        verification_tier=card.tier,
   172	        verification_score=card.score,
   173	        verifier_version=QUOTE_VERIFIER_VERSION,
   174	        source_chunk_id=uuid.UUID(card.chunk_id),
   175	        source_kind=card.source_kind,
   176	        # M3 review addition (plan §8.1 anchor fields, 2026-08-03): copied
   177	        # from the card as-is, same as every other trust field — None
   178	        # whenever the caller's card didn't come from verify_saved_quote()
   179	        # (the column is nullable for exactly that reason).
   180	        source_text_hash=card.source_text_hash,
   181	        quote_start=card.quote_start,
   182	        quote_end=card.quote_end,
   183	        quote_hash=quote_hash,
   184	        note=None,
   185	    )
   186	    db.add(row)
   187	    try:
   188	        await db.commit()
   189	    except IntegrityError:
   190	        await db.rollback()
   191	        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as retry_db:
   192	            winner = await get_existing_saved_quote(
   193	                retry_db, user_id=user.id, document_id=document.id, quote_hash=quote_hash
   194	            )
   195	        if winner is None:
   196	            raise  # not the race we anticipated — a genuine failure
   197	        return SaveQuoteOutcome(row=winner, created=False, limit_reached=False, active_count=active_count)
   198	    return SaveQuoteOutcome(row=row, created=True, limit_reached=False, active_count=active_count + 1)
   199	
   200	
   201	async def list_saved_quotes_for_document(
   202	    db: AsyncSession, *, user_id: uuid.UUID, document_id: uuid.UUID,
   203	) -> list[SavedQuote]:
   204	    result = await db.execute(
   205	        sa.select(SavedQuote)
   206	        .where(SavedQuote.user_id == user_id)
   207	        .where(SavedQuote.document_id == document_id)
   208	        .order_by(SavedQuote.created_at.desc())
   209	    )
   210	    return list(result.scalars().all())
   211	
   212	
   213	async def list_all_saved_quotes(db: AsyncSession, *, user_id: uuid.UUID) -> list[SavedQuote]:
   214	    """The Evidence Board feed — every saved quote for this user, across
   215	    every document, newest first. Uses idx_saved_quotes_user_created.
   216	
   217	    FIX-7-backend (Codex M3 r1 LOW): selectinload(SavedQuote.document) so
   218	    the API layer can read row.document.filename directly — a query-time
   219	    join, not a second round trip — for SavedQuoteBoardResponse's
   220	    document_filename field. The frontend's own document list is capped
   221	    (50 docs) and excludes demo docs, so it can't reliably resolve a
   222	    filename for every saved quote client-side."""
   223	    result = await db.execute(
   224	        sa.select(SavedQuote)
   225	        .where(SavedQuote.user_id == user_id)
   226	        .options(selectinload(SavedQuote.document))
   227	        .order_by(SavedQuote.created_at.desc())
   228	    )
   229	    return list(result.scalars().all())
   230	
   231	
   232	async def get_owned_saved_quote(
   233	    db: AsyncSession, *, user_id: uuid.UUID, saved_quote_id: uuid.UUID,
   234	) -> Optional[SavedQuote]:
   235	    """Ownership is baked directly into the WHERE clause (sharing.py's
   236	    revoke_share precedent) — a saved quote belonging to another user
   237	    simply doesn't match, so callers get a uniform 404 for both
   238	    "doesn't exist" and "exists but isn't yours," never leaking which."""
   239	    result = await db.execute(
   240	        sa.select(SavedQuote)
   241	        .where(SavedQuote.id == saved_quote_id)
   242	        .where(SavedQuote.user_id == user_id)
   243	    )
   244	    return result.scalar_one_or_none()
   245	
   246	
   247	async def update_note(db: AsyncSession, *, row: SavedQuote, note: Optional[str]) -> SavedQuote:
   248	    """Live E2E bug (team lead, 2026-08-03): PATCH .../quotes/{id} 500'd
   249	    with `sqlalchemy.exc.MissingGreenlet`. Root cause: `updated_at`
   250	    (server-side `onupdate=sa.func.now()`) gets marked EXPIRED by
   251	    SQLAlchemy after this UPDATE's flush — UNLIKE a fresh INSERT (see
   252	    save_quote() above), where INSERT...RETURNING auto-populates
   253	    server_default columns synchronously as part of the flush, an UPDATE's
   254	    onupdate-computed value is NOT auto-refreshed the same way. The
   255	    caller's later SYNCHRONOUS read of that expired attribute
   256	    (`row.updated_at.isoformat()` inside quotes.py's _saved_quote_response,
   257	    which runs as plain Pydantic-model construction, never inside an
   258	    `await`) triggered an implicit lazy DB reload from OUTSIDE an active
   259	    greenlet/await context — exactly MissingGreenlet. `db.refresh(row)`
   260	    here, INSIDE this awaited function, forces that reload to happen
   261	    safely now, so every attribute is a normal in-memory value by the time
   262	    any caller touches it synchronously. Mocked unit tests (row as a bare
   263	    SimpleNamespace) cannot exercise this at all — only a REAL SQLAlchemy
   264	    ORM object has attribute-expiration machinery to trigger it; see
   265	    test_saved_quotes_integration.py's TestSavedQuotesEndpointsRealAsgiRealDb
   266	    for the real-app/real-DB regression coverage."""
   267	    row.note = note
   268	    await db.commit()
   269	    await db.refresh(row)
   270	    return row
   271	
   272	
   273	async def delete_saved_quote(db: AsyncSession, *, row: SavedQuote) -> None:
   274	    await db.delete(row)
   275	    await db.commit()

   786	    saved quotes must survive/revalidate after reparses (§8.1), so the
   787	    display path reads these stored columns and NEVER re-runs verify_quote.
   788	    `source_chunk_id` is therefore ON DELETE SET NULL, not CASCADE: the
   789	    parse worker hard-deletes and recreates a document's chunks on every
   790	    reparse (parse_worker.py's `sa_delete(Chunk).where(...)`), and a saved
   791	    quote must outlive that — losing only the (informational) live chunk
   792	    link, never the row itself or its snapshotted display fields.
   793	
   794	    Cap enforcement (§8.4 point 2) counts ACTIVE rows per user ACROSS
   795	    documents — there is no soft-delete flag; DELETE is a real row removal,

+"""Real-Postgres integration test for the GDPR data-portability export
+(FIX-2, Codex M3 r1 MED): GET /api/users/me/export omitted saved_quotes
+entirely — including user-authored `note` text, which is personal data in
+its own right, not just a research artifact. Requires the real endpoint
+(rate-limit dict, real relationship eager-loading), so this is a real-app,
+real-scratch-DB test rather than a mocked one — a mocked `db` cannot
+convincingly fake `selectinload(SavedQuote.document)` eager loading.
+
+Requires docker (Postgres) — SKIP_INTEGRATION=1 (the default) skips this
+whole file.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import sys
+import uuid
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import pytest
+
+BACKEND_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+if str(BACKEND_DIR) not in sys.path:
+    sys.path.insert(0, str(BACKEND_DIR))
+
+pytestmark = [pytest.mark.integration, pytest.mark.asyncio(loop_scope="session")]
+
+
+async def _create_ready_document(user_id: uuid.UUID, *, filename: str) -> uuid.UUID:
+    from app.models.database import AsyncSessionLocal
+    from app.models.tables import Document
+
+    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
+        doc = Document(
+            filename=filename, file_size=100,
+            storage_key=f"documents/{uuid.uuid4()}/{filename}", status="ready", user_id=user_id,
+        )
+        db.add(doc)
+        await db.commit()
+        await db.refresh(doc)
+        return doc.id
+
+
+async def _create_chunk(document_id: uuid.UUID) -> uuid.UUID:
+    from app.models.database import AsyncSessionLocal
+    from app.models.tables import Chunk
+
+    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
+        chunk = Chunk(
+            document_id=document_id, chunk_index=0, text="hello world", token_count=2,
+            page_start=1, page_end=1, bboxes=[],
+        )
+        db.add(chunk)
+        await db.commit()
+        await db.refresh(chunk)
+        return chunk.id
+
+
+class TestGdprExportIncludesSavedQuotes:
+    async def test_export_includes_saved_quotes_with_note_and_document_filename(
+        self, client, auth_user, auth_headers,
+    ) -> None:
+        from app.models.database import AsyncSessionLocal
+        from app.models.tables import Document
+        from app.services import saved_quotes_service
+        from app.services.quote_search_service import QuoteCard
+
+        document_id = await _create_ready_document(auth_user.id, filename="export-test.pdf")
+        chunk_id = await _create_chunk(document_id)
+        card = QuoteCard(
+            display_text="the exact quoted sentence", page=4, page_end=4, bboxes=[],
+            tier="exact", source_kind="extracted_text", chunk_id=str(chunk_id), score=100.0,
+        )
+        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
+            document = await db.get(Document, document_id)
+            outcome = await saved_quotes_service.save_quote(
+                db, user=auth_user, document=document, card=card,
+            )
+            assert outcome.created is True
+            row = await saved_quotes_service.update_note(
+                db, row=outcome.row, note="cite this in the introduction — personal working note",
+            )
+            saved_quote_id = row.id
+
+        response = await client.get("/api/users/me/export", headers=auth_headers)
+
+        assert response.status_code == 200
+        body = response.json()
+        assert "saved_quotes" in body
+        quotes = body["saved_quotes"]
+        assert len(quotes) == 1
+        exported = quotes[0]
+        assert exported["id"] == str(saved_quote_id)
+        assert exported["quote_text"] == "the exact quoted sentence"
+        assert exported["note"] == "cite this in the introduction — personal working note"
+        assert exported["document_filename"] == "export-test.pdf"
+        assert exported["page"] == 4
+        assert exported["created_at"] is not None
+
+    async def test_export_saved_quotes_section_is_empty_list_when_user_has_none(
+        self, client, auth_user, auth_headers,
+    ) -> None:
+        response = await client.get("/api/users/me/export", headers=auth_headers)
+        assert response.status_code == 200
+        assert response.json()["saved_quotes"] == []

 """
 from __future__ import annotations

 from httpx import ASGITransport, AsyncClient
 
 from app.api import quotes as quotes_api
 from app.core import deps as deps_module
 from app.core.config import settings
 from app.services import saved_quotes_service
 from app.services.quote_search_service import QuoteCard
 
 api_app = FastAPI()
 api_app.include_router(quotes_api.router)
 
 
 def _make_user(*, plan: str = "free") -> SimpleNamespace:
     return SimpleNamespace(id=uuid.uuid4(), plan=plan, email="user@example.com")
 
 
 def _make_doc(user: SimpleNamespace, *, status: str = "ready", demo_slug=None) -> SimpleNamespace:
     return SimpleNamespace(id=uuid.uuid4(), user_id=user.id, status=status, demo_slug=demo_slug)
 
 
 def _make_db(**overrides: object) -> SimpleNamespace:
     payload: dict[str, object] = {
         "get": AsyncMock(return_value=None),
         "execute": AsyncMock(),
         "add": lambda _obj: None,
         "commit": AsyncMock(),
         "rollback": AsyncMock(),
     }
     payload.update(overrides)
     return SimpleNamespace(**payload)
 
 
 def _override_dependencies(db: object, user: object) -> None:
     async def _get_db():
         yield db
 
     async def _require_auth():
         return user
 
     api_app.dependency_overrides[deps_module.get_db_session] = _get_db
     api_app.dependency_overrides[deps_module.require_auth] = _require_auth
 
 
 def _sample_card(**overrides) -> QuoteCard:
     base = dict(
         display_text="the exact quoted sentence",
         page=4, page_end=4, bboxes=[{"x": 0.1, "y": 0.2, "w": 0.3, "h": 0.05, "page": 4}],
         tier="exact", source_kind="extracted_text", chunk_id=str(uuid.uuid4()), score=100.0,
     )
     base.update(overrides)
     return QuoteCard(**base)
 
 
 def _saved_row(**overrides) -> SimpleNamespace:
     now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
     base = dict(
         id=uuid.uuid4(), document_id=uuid.uuid4(), page=4, page_end=4,
         quote_text="the exact quoted sentence",
         bboxes=[{"x": 0.1, "y": 0.2, "w": 0.3, "h": 0.05, "page": 4}],
         verification_tier="exact", verification_score=100.0, verifier_version="v1",
         source_kind="extracted_text", note=None, created_at=now, updated_at=now,
+        # FIX-7-backend (Codex M3 r1 LOW): _saved_quote_board_response
+        # reads row.document.filename (list_all_saved_quotes'
+        # selectinload) — a plain default here so every test using this
+        # helper works with the board endpoint without needing to opt in.
+        document=SimpleNamespace(filename="test-document.pdf"),
     )
     base.update(overrides)
     return SimpleNamespace(**base)
 
 
 def _assert_error(response, status_code: int, error_code: str) -> dict:
     assert response.status_code == status_code
     body = response.json()
     detail = body["detail"]
     assert detail["error"] == error_code
     return detail
 
 
 @pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
 def _clear_dependency_overrides() -> None:
     api_app.dependency_overrides.clear()
     yield
     api_app.dependency_overrides.clear()
 
 
 @pytest_asyncio.fixture
 async def client():
     async with AsyncClient(transport=ASGITransport(app=api_app), base_url="http://test") as ac:
         yield ac
 
 
 # -------------------------- POST create --------------------------
 
 @pytest.mark.asyncio
 async def test_create_requires_auth(client: AsyncClient) -> None:
     document_id = uuid.uuid4()
     response = await client.post(
         f"/api/documents/{document_id}/quotes",
         json={"chunk_id": str(uuid.uuid4()), "quote_text": "x"},
     )
     assert response.status_code == 401
 
 
 @pytest.mark.asyncio
 async def test_create_rejects_inaccessible_document(client: AsyncClient) -> None:
     user = _make_user()
     other_user_doc = _make_doc(_make_user())
     db = _make_db(get=AsyncMock(return_value=other_user_doc))
     _override_dependencies(db, user)
 
     response = await client.post(
         f"/api/documents/{other_user_doc.id}/quotes",
         json={"chunk_id": str(uuid.uuid4()), "quote_text": "x"},
     )
 
     _assert_error(response, 404, "DOCUMENT_NOT_FOUND")
 
 
 @pytest.mark.asyncio
 async def test_create_rejects_malformed_chunk_id(client: AsyncClient) -> None:
     user = _make_user()
     doc = _make_doc(user)
     db = _make_db(get=AsyncMock(return_value=doc))
     _override_dependencies(db, user)
 
     response = await client.post(
         f"/api/documents/{doc.id}/quotes",
         json={"chunk_id": "not-a-uuid", "quote_text": "x"},
     )
 
     _assert_error(response, 422, "INVALID_CHUNK_ID")
 
 
 @pytest.mark.asyncio
 async def test_create_rejects_a_quote_that_fails_reverification(
     client: AsyncClient, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
 ) -> None:
     """The core anti-fabrication guarantee at the HTTP boundary: a client
     posting text that verify_saved_quote cannot locate gets a hard 422,
     never a silently-persisted "verified" row."""
     user = _make_user()
     doc = _make_doc(user)
     db = _make_db(get=AsyncMock(return_value=doc))
     _override_dependencies(db, user)
     monkeypatch.setattr(
         quotes_api.quote_search_service, "verify_saved_quote", AsyncMock(return_value=None)
     )
     save_mock = AsyncMock()
     monkeypatch.setattr(saved_quotes_service, "save_quote", save_mock)
 
     response = await client.post(
         f"/api/documents/{doc.id}/quotes",
         json={"chunk_id": str(uuid.uuid4()), "quote_text": "I confess to fraud"},
     )
 
     _assert_error(response, 422, "QUOTE_NOT_VERIFIABLE")
     save_mock.assert_not_awaited()

 @pytest.mark.asyncio
 async def test_patch_updates_note_only(
     client: AsyncClient, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
 ) -> None:
     user = _make_user()
     db = _make_db()
     _override_dependencies(db, user)
     row = _saved_row(note=None)
     monkeypatch.setattr(saved_quotes_service, "get_owned_saved_quote", AsyncMock(return_value=row))
 
     async def _fake_update_note(_db, *, row, note):
         row.note = note
         return row
 
     monkeypatch.setattr(saved_quotes_service, "update_note", _fake_update_note)
 
     response = await client.patch(f"/api/quotes/{row.id}", json={"note": "cite this in intro"})
 
     assert response.status_code == 200
     assert response.json()["note"] == "cite this in intro"

 def _card(**overrides) -> QuoteCard:
     base = dict(

 class _ScalarResult:
     def __init__(self, value=None, values=None):
         self._value = value
         self._values = values if values is not None else ([] if value is None else [value])
 
     def scalar_one_or_none(self):
         return self._value
 
     def scalar(self):
         return self._value
 
     def scalars(self):
         return SimpleNamespace(all=lambda: self._values)
 
 
 class TestSavedQuotesLimitForPlan:
     def test_free_plan_uses_free_limit(self) -> None:
         assert sqs.saved_quotes_limit_for_plan("free") == settings.FREE_SAVED_QUOTES_LIMIT
 
     def test_plus_plan_uses_plus_limit(self) -> None:
         assert sqs.saved_quotes_limit_for_plan("plus") == settings.PLUS_SAVED_QUOTES_LIMIT
 
     def test_pro_plan_uses_pro_limit(self) -> None:
         assert sqs.saved_quotes_limit_for_plan("pro") == settings.PRO_SAVED_QUOTES_LIMIT
 
     def test_missing_or_unknown_plan_defaults_to_free(self) -> None:
         assert sqs.saved_quotes_limit_for_plan(None) == settings.FREE_SAVED_QUOTES_LIMIT
         assert sqs.saved_quotes_limit_for_plan("enterprise") == settings.FREE_SAVED_QUOTES_LIMIT
 
     def test_case_insensitive(self) -> None:
         assert sqs.saved_quotes_limit_for_plan("PRO") == settings.PRO_SAVED_QUOTES_LIMIT
 
 
 class TestComputeQuoteHash:
     def test_deterministic_for_the_same_inputs(self) -> None:
         h1 = sqs.compute_quote_hash("Hello world.", 3, 3)
         h2 = sqs.compute_quote_hash("Hello world.", 3, 3)
         assert h1 == h2
 
     def test_normalizes_whitespace_and_quote_variants(self) -> None:
         """§8.1's dedup philosophy: two honest saves of the "same" wording
         that differ only in incidental formatting (smart quotes, extra
         whitespace) must collide, or the unique constraint silently stops
         protecting against duplicate saves."""
         h1 = sqs.compute_quote_hash("“Hello   world.”", 3, 3)
         h2 = sqs.compute_quote_hash('"Hello world."', 3, 3)
         assert h1 == h2
 
     def test_different_page_range_changes_the_hash(self) -> None:
         """A client cannot dodge the unique constraint by claiming a fake
         page for identical text, NOR can the same real text on two
         genuinely different pages ever incorrectly collide."""
         h1 = sqs.compute_quote_hash("Hello world.", 3, 3)
         h2 = sqs.compute_quote_hash("Hello world.", 4, 4)
         assert h1 != h2
 
     def test_different_text_changes_the_hash(self) -> None:
         h1 = sqs.compute_quote_hash("Hello world.", 3, 3)
         h2 = sqs.compute_quote_hash("Goodbye world.", 3, 3)
         assert h1 != h2

         because `updated_at` (onupdate=sa.func.now()) gets marked EXPIRED
         after the UPDATE flush and was read synchronously afterward
         (quotes.py's _saved_quote_response) outside an awaited context.

         caller touches it."""

         assert calls == ["commit", "refresh"]

 """Real-Postgres integration tests for saved_quotes (M3-B1/B2/B3, plan D8
 amended by §8.5 M3 / §8.1 / §8.4 point 2).
 
 Mocked-db unit tests (test_saved_quotes_service.py, test_saved_quotes_api.py)

 database — a genuine UNIQUE (user_id, document_id, quote_hash) index under
 real concurrent inserts, and the ON DELETE SET NULL FK actually surviving a
 real chunk deletion (§8.1's "saved quotes must survive reparses"
 requirement, reproduced here as the parse worker's own deletion shape:

 whole file.

 import pytest
 from sqlalchemy import delete as sa_delete

 BACKEND_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
 if str(BACKEND_DIR) not in sys.path:
     sys.path.insert(0, str(BACKEND_DIR))
 
 pytestmark = [pytest.mark.integration, pytest.mark.asyncio(loop_scope="session")]
 
 
 async def _create_ready_document(user_id: uuid.UUID) -> uuid.UUID:
     from app.models.database import AsyncSessionLocal
     from app.models.tables import Document
 
     async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
         doc = Document(
             filename="integration-test.pdf",
             file_size=100,
             storage_key=f"documents/{uuid.uuid4()}/integration-test.pdf",
             status="ready",
             user_id=user_id,
         )
         db.add(doc)
         await db.commit()
         await db.refresh(doc)
         return doc.id
 
 
 async def _create_chunk(document_id: uuid.UUID) -> uuid.UUID:
     from app.models.database import AsyncSessionLocal
     from app.models.tables import Chunk
 
     async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
         chunk = Chunk(
             document_id=document_id, chunk_index=0,
             text="Fluency is the most prized quality in translation today.",
             token_count=12, page_start=4, page_end=4, bboxes=[],
         )
         db.add(chunk)
         await db.commit()
         await db.refresh(chunk)
         return chunk.id
 
 
 async def _saved_quote_rows(user_id: uuid.UUID):
     from app.models.database import AsyncSessionLocal
     from app.models.tables import SavedQuote
 
     async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
         result = await db.execute(select(SavedQuote).where(SavedQuote.user_id == user_id))
         return list(result.scalars().all())
 
 
 def _card(chunk_id: uuid.UUID):
     from app.services.quote_search_service import QuoteCard
 
     return QuoteCard(
         display_text="the most prized quality in translation today",
         page=4, page_end=4, bboxes=[], tier="exact", source_kind="extracted_text",
         chunk_id=str(chunk_id), score=100.0,
     )
 
 
 class TestConcurrentIdenticalSaveNeverDuplicates:
     async def test_two_concurrent_identical_saves_produce_exactly_one_row(
         self, auth_user,
     ) -> None:
         """Codex-style deterministic-race reproduction: two requests for the
         SAME quote (double-click, two tabs) both racing to INSERT. Mocked
         tests can prove the retry LOGIC runs; only a real unique index can
         prove the DATABASE actually stops the duplicate.
 
         `return_exceptions=True` + a final settle step is deliberate, not a
         weakened assertion: this project's test suite runs against NullPool
         (TESTING=1, see app/models/database.py) so every checkout opens a
         brand-new physical connection, and a truly concurrent
         rollback-then-new-connection sequence under asyncio.gather can
         surface a transient SQLAlchemy/asyncpg async-bridge hiccup
         (`MissingGreenlet`) that is specific to NullPool under test —
         reproduced standalone outside pytest, and confirmed ABSENT against
         a production-shaped QueuePool-backed engine (the same pool
         app/models/database.py uses whenever TESTING is unset). Treating
         that as an acceptable outcome for a racing side, while still
         requiring the DATABASE to end up in exactly one of the two
         correct end-states every single round, is what actually matters:
         the unique index is the thing under test, not this harness's pool
         implementation."""
         from app.models.database import AsyncSessionLocal
         from app.services import saved_quotes_service
 
         document_id = await _create_ready_document(auth_user.id)
         chunk_id = await _create_chunk(document_id)
         card = _card(chunk_id)
         expected_hash = saved_quotes_service.compute_quote_hash(card.display_text, card.page, card.page_end)
 
         async def _one_save():
             from app.models.tables import Document
 
             async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
                 document = await db.get(Document, document_id)
                 return await saved_quotes_service.save_quote(
                     db, user=auth_user, document=document, card=card,
                 )
 
         for _round in range(5):
             results = await asyncio.gather(_one_save(), _one_save(), return_exceptions=True)
             clean = [r for r in results if not isinstance(r, BaseException)]
             # At least one side must land cleanly — a genuine failure on
             # BOTH sides simultaneously would mean the row never got saved
             # at all, which the settle step below cannot paper over.
             assert clean, f"round {_round}: both concurrent saves raised: {results!r}"
 
             # Whichever side (if either) hit the NullPool artifact above,
             # settle deterministically: a plain, NON-concurrent save must
             # always resolve to the SAME row via the idempotent path.
-            settled_row, _settled_created = await _one_save()
+            settled_outcome = await _one_save()
+            settled_row = settled_outcome.row
 
             rows = await _saved_quote_rows(auth_user.id)
             assert len(rows) == 1, f"round {_round}: expected exactly one row, got {len(rows)}"
             assert rows[0].quote_hash == expected_hash
             assert rows[0].id == settled_row.id
-            for row, _created in clean:
-                assert row.id == settled_row.id  # every clean result agreed on the SAME row
+            for outcome in clean:
+                assert outcome.row.id == settled_row.id  # every clean result agreed on the SAME row
 
             # Clean up between rounds so each round starts from "no row yet."
             async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
                 from app.models.tables import SavedQuote
 
                 await db.execute(sa_delete(SavedQuote).where(SavedQuote.id == settled_row.id))
                 await db.commit()
-        assert rows[0].quote_hash == saved_quotes_service.compute_quote_hash(
-            card.display_text, card.page, card.page_end
-        )
 
 
 class TestSavedQuoteSurvivesChunkDeletion:
     async def test_reparse_style_chunk_deletion_leaves_the_saved_row_intact(
         self, auth_user,
     ) -> None:
         """§8.1: saved quotes must survive reparses. Reproduces the parse
         worker's own deletion shape (parse_worker.py:184,
         `sa_delete(Chunk).where(Chunk.document_id == doc.id)`) directly —
         the FK's ON DELETE SET NULL must leave the saved_quotes row (and
         every one of its snapshotted display fields) untouched, losing only
         the now-dangling chunk reference."""
         from app.models.database import AsyncSessionLocal
         from app.models.tables import Chunk, Document, SavedQuote
         from app.services import saved_quotes_service
 
         document_id = await _create_ready_document(auth_user.id)
         chunk_id = await _create_chunk(document_id)
         card = _card(chunk_id)
 
         async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
             document = await db.get(Document, document_id)
-            row, created = await saved_quotes_service.save_quote(
+            outcome = await saved_quotes_service.save_quote(
                 db, user=auth_user, document=document, card=card,
             )
-            assert created is True
-            saved_quote_id = row.id
+            assert outcome.created is True
+            saved_quote_id = outcome.row.id
 
         # Reproduce the parse worker's reparse-time chunk deletion exactly.
         async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
             await db.execute(sa_delete(Chunk).where(Chunk.document_id == document_id))
             await db.commit()
 
         async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
             survivor = await db.get(SavedQuote, saved_quote_id)
             assert survivor is not None
             assert survivor.source_chunk_id is None  # SET NULL, not a dangling reference
             # Every snapshotted display field is untouched by the chunk's death.
             assert survivor.quote_text == card.display_text
             assert survivor.page == card.page
             assert survivor.page_end == card.page_end
             assert survivor.verification_tier == card.tier
             assert survivor.verification_score == card.score
             assert survivor.source_kind == card.source_kind
 
 
 class TestSavedQuotesEndpointsRealAsgiRealDb:
     """Real-server bug found by live E2E (team lead, 2026-08-03): PATCH
     /api/quotes/{id} -> 500 MissingGreenlet. Root cause: SavedQuote.updated_at
     (server-side `onupdate=sa.func.now()`) gets marked EXPIRED by SQLAlchemy
     after the UPDATE flush inside saved_quotes_service.update_note() —
     UNLIKE a fresh INSERT (save_quote's POST path), where INSERT...RETURNING
     auto-populates server_default columns synchronously as part of the
     flush, an UPDATE's onupdate-computed value is NOT auto-refreshed the
     same way. The endpoint's later SYNCHRONOUS read of that expired
     attribute (`row.updated_at.isoformat()` inside _saved_quote_response)
     triggers an implicit lazy DB reload from OUTSIDE an active
     greenlet/await context — exactly `MissingGreenlet`.
 
     Every mocked unit test in test_saved_quotes_api.py used a bare
     SimpleNamespace for `row`, which has no SQLAlchemy attribute-expiration
     machinery at all — structurally incapable of catching this class of
     bug. These tests hit the REAL app (app.main.app) via a REAL httpx
     client and a REAL scratch-DB session per request (conftest.py's
     `client`/`auth_user`/`auth_headers` fixtures), reproducing the exact
     conditions of the live bug report."""
 
     async def test_full_lifecycle_via_real_http_never_hits_missing_greenlet(
         self, client, auth_user, auth_headers,
     ) -> None:
         document_id = await _create_ready_document(auth_user.id)
         chunk_id = await _create_chunk(document_id)
         card = _card(chunk_id)
 
         # POST: genuinely new save -> 201.
         create_response = await client.post(
             f"/api/documents/{document_id}/quotes",
             json={"chunk_id": str(chunk_id), "quote_text": card.display_text, "page_hint": card.page},
             headers=auth_headers,
         )
         assert create_response.status_code == 201
         body = create_response.json()
         saved_quote_id = body["id"]
         assert body["tier"] == "exact"
         assert body["note"] is None
 
         # POST again, identical: idempotent hit -> 200, NOT 201 (team lead's
         # contract-alignment finding: the plan says "returns the existing
         # row (200 not 409)" — 201 for every response was a deviation).
         repeat_response = await client.post(
             f"/api/documents/{document_id}/quotes",
             json={"chunk_id": str(chunk_id), "quote_text": card.display_text, "page_hint": card.page},
             headers=auth_headers,
         )
         assert repeat_response.status_code == 200
         assert repeat_response.json()["id"] == saved_quote_id
 
         # GET (document-scoped) -> 200, one row.
         list_doc_response = await client.get(
             f"/api/documents/{document_id}/quotes", headers=auth_headers,
         )
         assert list_doc_response.status_code == 200
         assert len(list_doc_response.json()["quotes"]) == 1
 
         # GET (Evidence Board, all documents) -> 200, includes this row.
         list_all_response = await client.get("/api/quotes", headers=auth_headers)
         assert list_all_response.status_code == 200
         assert any(q["id"] == saved_quote_id for q in list_all_response.json()["quotes"])
 
         # PATCH: THE bug repro. Must be 200, never 500.
         patch_response = await client.patch(
             f"/api/quotes/{saved_quote_id}", json={"note": "cite this in the intro"},
             headers=auth_headers,
         )
         assert patch_response.status_code == 200
         assert patch_response.json()["note"] == "cite this in the intro"
         assert patch_response.json()["id"] == saved_quote_id
 
         # DELETE -> 204.
         delete_response = await client.delete(
             f"/api/quotes/{saved_quote_id}", headers=auth_headers,
         )
         assert delete_response.status_code == 204
 
         # Confirm it's actually gone.
         list_after_delete = await client.get(
             f"/api/documents/{document_id}/quotes", headers=auth_headers,
         )
         assert list_after_delete.json()["quotes"] == []
 
     async def test_save_populates_verification_anchor_columns_end_to_end(
         self, client, auth_user, auth_headers,
     ) -> None:
         """M3 review addition (plan §8.1 anchor fields, 2026-08-03): a real
         POST through the full stack (endpoint -> verify_saved_quote ->
         save_quote) must land non-NULL source_text_hash/quote_start/
         quote_end in the actual row — not just in mocked-unit-test
         plumbing. Not yet exposed via the API response (backend-only,
         v1 never reads them back), so this reads the DB directly."""
         import hashlib
 
         from app.models.database import AsyncSessionLocal
         from app.models.tables import SavedQuote
 
         document_id = await _create_ready_document(auth_user.id)
         chunk_id = await _create_chunk(document_id)
         chunk_text = "Fluency is the most prized quality in translation today."
         quote_text = "the most prized quality in translation today"
 
         response = await client.post(
             f"/api/documents/{document_id}/quotes",
             json={"chunk_id": str(chunk_id), "quote_text": quote_text, "page_hint": 4},
             headers=auth_headers,
         )
         assert response.status_code == 201
 
         async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
             row = await db.get(SavedQuote, uuid.UUID(response.json()["id"]))
             assert row.source_text_hash == hashlib.sha256(chunk_text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
             assert row.quote_start == chunk_text.index(quote_text)
             assert row.quote_end == chunk_text.index(quote_text) + len(quote_text)
+
+
+class TestConcurrentDistinctSavesRespectTheCap:
+    """FIX-1 (Codex M3 r1 HIGH — cap race). Codex's exact finding: the old
+    count check ran in the API layer, OUTSIDE any lock and outside
+    save_quote() entirely — at limit-1 rows, concurrent DISTINCT saves
+    (different quote_hash, unlike TestConcurrentIdenticalSaveNeverDuplicates
+    above, which is about the SAME quote) all read "count < limit" and all
+    committed. Reproduced directly before this fix: 3 concurrent saves at
+    29/30 all succeeded, final count 32. Real HTTP, real scratch DB, real
+    concurrency — the team lead's exact scenario: 29 pre-existing rows,
+    concurrent distinct saves for the one remaining slot, exactly one
+    succeeds, final count 30, every loser gets the 403 shape."""
+
+    async def test_29_preexisting_rows_plus_concurrent_distinct_saves_yields_exactly_30(
+        self, client, auth_user, auth_headers,
+    ) -> None:
+        from app.core.config import settings
+        from app.models.database import AsyncSessionLocal
+        from app.models.tables import SavedQuote
+
+        document_id = await _create_ready_document(auth_user.id)
+        chunk_id = await _create_chunk(document_id)
+        chunk_text = "Fluency is the most prized quality in translation today."
+        limit = settings.FREE_SAVED_QUOTES_LIMIT
+
+        # Seed limit-1 pre-existing rows directly (bypassing verification —
+        # their CONTENT doesn't matter, only that they count toward the
+        # cap). One slot remains open.
+        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
+            for i in range(limit - 1):
+                db.add(SavedQuote(
+                    user_id=auth_user.id, document_id=document_id, page=1, page_end=1,
+                    quote_text=f"seed {i}", bboxes=None, verification_tier="exact",
+                    verification_score=100.0, verifier_version="v1", source_chunk_id=chunk_id,
+                    source_kind="extracted_text", quote_hash=f"seed-hash-{i}", note=None,
+                ))
+            await db.commit()
+
+        # Three DISTINCT (genuinely different, all independently verifiable)
+        # substrings of the SAME real chunk text — racing for the one open slot.
+        distinct_quotes = ["Fluency is", "the most prized quality", "in translation today"]
+        assert all(q in chunk_text for q in distinct_quotes)  # sanity: all real substrings
+
+        async def _one_post(quote_text: str):
+            return await client.post(
+                f"/api/documents/{document_id}/quotes",
+                json={"chunk_id": str(chunk_id), "quote_text": quote_text},
+                headers=auth_headers,
+            )
+
+        responses = await asyncio.gather(*[_one_post(q) for q in distinct_quotes])
+
+        succeeded = [r for r in responses if r.status_code == 201]
+        rejected = [r for r in responses if r.status_code == 403]
+        assert len(succeeded) == 1
+        assert len(rejected) == 2
+        for r in rejected:
+            body = r.json()
+            assert body["detail"]["error"] == "SAVED_QUOTES_LIMIT_REACHED"
+            assert body["detail"]["limit"] == limit
+            assert body["detail"]["plan"] == "free"
+
+        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
+            final_count = await db.scalar(
+                select(func.count()).select_from(SavedQuote).where(SavedQuote.user_id == auth_user.id)
+            )
+        assert final_count == limit  # never 31+, the cap held under real concurrency
+
+
+class TestBoardFeedIncludesDocumentFilename:
+    """FIX-7-backend (Codex M3 r1 LOW): GET /api/quotes (the Evidence
+    Board feed) gains document_filename per row, joined at query time —
+    real end-to-end proof (mocked-db can't convincingly fake
+    selectinload eager-loading), not exposed on the document-scoped
+    endpoints (POST/PATCH/per-document GET)."""
+
+    async def test_board_feed_rows_carry_the_real_document_filename(
+        self, client, auth_user, auth_headers,
+    ) -> None:
+        document_id = await _create_ready_document(auth_user.id)
+        chunk_id = await _create_chunk(document_id)
+        card = _card(chunk_id)
+
+        from app.models.database import AsyncSessionLocal
+        from app.models.tables import Document
+        from app.services import saved_quotes_service
+
+        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
+            document = await db.get(Document, document_id)
+            outcome = await saved_quotes_service.save_quote(
+                db, user=auth_user, document=document, card=card,
+            )
+            assert outcome.created is True
+            real_filename = document.filename
+
+        response = await client.get("/api/quotes", headers=auth_headers)
+
+        assert response.status_code == 200
+        quotes = response.json()["quotes"]
+        assert len(quotes) == 1
+        assert quotes[0]["document_filename"] == real_filename
+
+        # The document-scoped list endpoint does NOT carry this field —
+        # confirms the field is genuinely board-specific, not accidentally
+        # shared onto the other response shape.
+        doc_scoped = await client.get(
+            f"/api/documents/{document_id}/quotes", headers=auth_headers,
+        )
+        assert "document_filename" not in doc_scoped.json()["quotes"][0]

   331	                </p>

   333	
   334	              {result && result.cards.length === 0 ? (
   335	                <p className="rounded-lg border border-[var(--reader-border)] bg-[var(--reader-panel-muted)] px-3 py-3 text-sm leading-6 text-[var(--reader-muted)]" role="status">
   336	                  {tOr(
   337	                    'quoteFinder.emptyState',
   338	                    "No verified quotes found for this topic (scanned {n} passages). DocTalk only shows quotes it can verify against the source text — try a more specific topic.",
   339	                    { n: result.scannedChunks },
   340	                  )}
   341	                </p>
   342	              ) : null}
   343	
   344	              {result && result.cards.length > 0 ? (
   345	                <QuoteCardList
   346	                  documentId={documentId}
   347	                  cards={result.cards}
   348	                  onJump={handleJump}
   349	                  allowEditBiblio
   350	                  userPlan={userPlan}
   351	                  summaryLine={tOr(
   352	                    'quoteFinder.resultsSummary',
   353	                    '{verified} verified · {discarded} discarded',
   354	                    { verified: result.verified, discarded: result.discardedCount },
   355	                  )}
   356	                />
   357	              ) : null}
   358	            </>
   359	          ) : (
   360	            <>
   361	              {savedErrorMsg ? (
   362	                <p className="mb-3 rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-red-800 dark:border-red-900/50 dark:bg-red-950/30 dark:text-red-200" role="alert">
   363	                  {savedErrorMsg}
   364	                </p>
   365	              ) : null}
   366	
   367	              {savedLoading ? (
   368	                <p className="text-sm text-[var(--reader-muted)]">{tOr('quoteFinder.savedLoading', 'Loading saved quotes...')}</p>
   369	              ) : savedQuotes && savedQuotes.length === 0 ? (
   370	                <p className="rounded-lg border border-[var(--reader-border)] bg-[var(--reader-panel-muted)] px-3 py-3 text-sm leading-6 text-[var(--reader-muted)]">
   371	                  {tOr('quoteFinder.savedEmpty', 'No saved quotes for this document yet. Save a verified quote from the Search tab to build your evidence board.')}
   372	                </p>
   373	              ) : savedQuotes && savedQuotes.length > 0 ? (
   374	                <SavedQuoteList
   375	                  documentId={documentId}
   376	                  quotes={savedQuotes}
   377	                  onJump={handleSavedJump}
   378	                  onDeleted={handleSavedDeleted}
   379	                  onNoteUpdated={handleSavedNoteUpdated}
   380	                  capLine={savedGlobalCount !== null
   381	                    ? tOr('quoteFinder.capIndicator', '{count} of {limit} saved', { count: savedGlobalCount, limit: FREE_SAVED_QUOTES_LIMIT })
   382	                    : undefined}
   383	                />
   384	              ) : null}
   385	            </>
   386	          )}
   387	        </div>
   388	      </div>
   389	
   390	      <PaywallModal isOpen={paywallOpen} onClose={() => setPaywallOpen(false)} reason={paywallReason} currentPlan={userPlan} />
   391	    </div>
   392	  );
   393	}

   272	// --- Quote Finder API (M2, plan §8.4.1 / §8.4.4) ---

   295	export async function searchDocumentQuotes(documentId: string, topic: string, locale?: string | null): Promise<QuoteSearchResult> {

   298	    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
   299	    body: JSON.stringify({ topic, locale: locale || null }),
   300	  });

   305	      page: c.page,
   306	      pageEnd: c.page_end,
   307	      bboxes: c.bboxes || [],
   308	      tier: c.tier,
   309	      sourceKind: c.source_kind,
   310	      chunkId: c.chunk_id,
   311	      score: c.score,
   312	    })),
   313	    proposed: data.proposed,
   314	    verified: data.verified,
   315	    discardedCount: data.discarded_count,
   316	    scannedChunks: data.scanned_chunks,
   317	    remainingCredits: data.remaining_credits,
   318	  };

   321	// --- Document biblio API (M2, plan §8.4.4 — minimal per-user citation metadata) ---
   322	
   323	export interface CslAuthor {
   324	  family?: string;

   333	}

   337	  source: 'system' | 'user' | string;

   340	export async function getDocumentBiblio(documentId: string): Promise<DocumentBiblio> {
   341	  const res = await fetch(`${PROXY_BASE}/api/documents/${documentId}/biblio`);
   342	  const data: any = await handle(res);
   343	  return { cslJson: data.csl_json || {}, source: data.source };

   347	  const res = await fetch(`${PROXY_BASE}/api/documents/${documentId}/biblio`, {

   356	// --- Saved Quotes API (M3, plan §8.5 / §8.4 point 2 — Evidence Board) ---
   357	
   358	export interface SavedQuote {
   359	  id: string;
   360	  documentId: string;
   361	  page: number;
   362	  pageEnd: number;
   363	  quoteText: string;
   364	  bboxes: NormalizedBBox[];
   365	  tier: 'exact' | 'normalized' | 'aligned' | string;
   366	  score: number;
   367	  verifierVersion: string;
   368	  sourceKind: 'page_text' | 'extracted_text' | string;
   369	  note: string | null;
   370	  createdAt: string;
   371	  updatedAt: string;
   372	  /** Only present on GET /api/quotes (the all-documents Evidence Board
   373	   * feed) — the backend calls this a distinct response shape
   374	   * (SavedQuoteBoardResponse vs SavedQuoteResponse) precisely because the
   375	   * document-scoped endpoints already know which document they're
   376	   * looking at and don't carry it. Codex M3 r1 FIX-7: resolves a saved
   377	   * quote's document name server-side (query-time join) instead of the
   378	   * frontend cross-referencing its own, capped-at-50/demo-excluding
   379	   * getMyDocuments() list. */
   380	  documentFilename?: string;
   381	}
   382	
   383	function mapSavedQuote(data: any): SavedQuote {
   384	  return {
   385	    id: data.id,
   386	    documentId: data.document_id,
   387	    page: data.page,
   388	    pageEnd: data.page_end,
   389	    quoteText: data.quote_text,
   390	    bboxes: data.bboxes || [],
   391	    tier: data.tier,
   392	    score: data.score,
   393	    verifierVersion: data.verifier_version,
   394	    sourceKind: data.source_kind,
   395	    note: data.note ?? null,
   396	    createdAt: data.created_at,
   397	    updatedAt: data.updated_at,
   398	    documentFilename: typeof data.document_filename === 'string' ? data.document_filename : undefined,
   399	  };
   400	}
   401	
   402	/**
   403	 * Saves a verified quote card. The server does NOT trust chunkId/quoteText
   404	 * for tier/score/page/bboxes — it re-derives every trust field itself
   405	 * (`quote_search_service.verify_saved_quote`), so only send back what a
   406	 * `QuoteCard` already gave you: `chunkId`, `displayText` -> quoteText, and
   407	 * optionally `page` as a disambiguation hint (never trusted to fabricate a
   408	 * page on its own). Idempotent — re-saving an already-saved quote returns
   409	 * 201 with the EXISTING row, never a duplicate and never a 409, so the
   410	 * caller doesn't need to track save-state itself before calling this.
   411	 */
   412	export async function saveQuote(documentId: string, params: { chunkId: string; quoteText: string; pageHint?: number }): Promise<SavedQuote> {
   413	  const res = await fetch(`${PROXY_BASE}/api/documents/${documentId}/quotes`, {
   414	    method: 'POST',
   415	    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
   416	    body: JSON.stringify({
   417	      chunk_id: params.chunkId,
   418	      quote_text: params.quoteText,
   419	      page_hint: params.pageHint ?? null,
   420	    }),
   421	  });
   422	  const data: any = await handle(res);
   423	  return mapSavedQuote(data);
   424	}
   425	
   426	/** One document's saved quotes, newest first. */
   427	export async function listDocumentSavedQuotes(documentId: string): Promise<SavedQuote[]> {
   428	  const res = await fetch(`${PROXY_BASE}/api/documents/${documentId}/quotes`);
   429	  const data: any = await handle(res);
   430	  return (Array.isArray(data.quotes) ? data.quotes : []).map(mapSavedQuote);

    31	export default function SavedQuoteCard({ quote, index, biblio, onJump, onDeleted, onNoteUpdated }: SavedQuoteCardProps) {
    32	  const { tOr } = useLocale();

    37	  // r1 finding #3). The prop only updates once the parent re-renders with
    38	  // the bubbled-up `onNoteUpdated` result, so comparing against it directly
    39	  // caused a redundant PATCH on every blur even when nothing had changed
    40	  // since the last successful save, and reverted a failed save to the
    41	  // pre-first-save value instead of the last confirmed one.
    42	  const [confirmedNote, setConfirmedNote] = useState(quote.note || '');
    43	  const [savingNote, setSavingNote] = useState(false);
    44	  const [noteError, setNoteError] = useState(false);
    45	  const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState(false);

    47	  // captured generation against the current one before applying ANY state,
    48	  // so an older overlapping save (e.g. blur, quick re-edit, blur again
    49	  // before the first PATCH lands) can never stomp a newer save's result —
    50	  // same compare-on-resolve pattern already used in QuoteFinderPanel /
    51	  // useChatStream for the identical class of race.
    52	  const noteSaveGenerationRef = useRef(0);

    54	  // The quote object is the parent's source of truth; re-sync the local

   111	      }

   113	    }
   114	  };

   117	    <div className="rounded-lg border border-[var(--reader-border)] bg-[var(--reader-panel-solid)] px-4 py-3">

   127	          }

   129	          {trustLabel(quote.sourceKind, tOr)}
   130	        </span>
   131	      </div>
   132	
   133	      <blockquote className="mt-2 border-l-2 border-blue-600 pl-3 text-sm italic leading-relaxed text-[var(--reader-ink)] dark:border-blue-400">
   134	        &ldquo;{quote.quoteText}&rdquo;

   136	
   137	      <div className="mt-2">

   186	          </button>

     1	"use client";
     2	
     3	import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
     4	import type { DocumentBiblioCsl, SavedQuote } from '../../lib/api';
     5	import { getDocumentBiblio } from '../../lib/api';
     6	import SavedQuoteCard from './SavedQuoteCard';
     7	
     8	interface SavedQuoteListProps {
     9	  documentId: string;
    10	  quotes: SavedQuote[];
    11	  onJump: (quote: SavedQuote, index: number) => void;
    12	  onDeleted: (quoteId: string) => void;
    13	  onNoteUpdated: (quote: SavedQuote) => void;
    14	  /** Localized "n of 30 saved" cap indicator (M3-F3), rendered above the
    15	   * list. Undefined on paid plans, which don't get the indicator. */
    16	  capLine?: string;
    17	}
    18	
    19	/**
    20	 * Per-document Evidence Board list (M3-F2) — the "Saved" tab/section
    21	 * inside the Quote Finder panel. Fetches this document's biblio once,
    22	 * same pattern as QuoteCardList, so every card's Copy action can append
    23	 * an APA in-text citation without a per-card round trip.
    24	 */
    25	export default function SavedQuoteList({ documentId, quotes, onJump, onDeleted, onNoteUpdated, capLine }: SavedQuoteListProps) {
    26	  const [biblio, setBiblio] = useState<DocumentBiblioCsl | null>(null);
    27	
    28	  useEffect(() => {
    29	    let cancelled = false;
    30	    setBiblio(null);
    31	    getDocumentBiblio(documentId)
    32	      .then((res) => {
    33	        if (!cancelled) setBiblio(res.cslJson);
    34	      })
    35	      .catch(() => {
    36	        if (!cancelled) setBiblio({});
    37	      });
    38	    return () => {
    39	      cancelled = true;
    40	    };
    41	  }, [documentId]);
    42	
    43	  return (
    44	    <div>
    45	      {capLine ? (
    46	        <p className="mb-2 px-1 text-xs text-[var(--reader-muted)]">{capLine}</p>
    47	      ) : null}
    48	      <div className="space-y-3">
    49	        {quotes.map((quote, index) => (
    50	          <SavedQuoteCard
    51	            key={quote.id}
    52	            quote={quote}
    53	            index={index}
    54	            biblio={biblio}
    55	            onJump={onJump}
    56	            onDeleted={onDeleted}
    57	            onNoteUpdated={onNoteUpdated}
    58	          />
    59	        ))}
    60	      </div>
    61	    </div>
    62	  );
    63	}

    36	# Estimated cost per mode for pre-debit (generous upper bound to avoid under-debit).
    37	# Internal mode IDs are kept for compatibility: quick=Flash, balanced=Pro.
    38	MODE_ESTIMATED_COST: dict[str, int] = {"quick": 5, "balanced": 15}
    39	
    40	
    41	def get_estimated_cost(mode: str) -> int:
    42	    """Return estimated credit cost for a mode (used for pre-debit)."""
    43	    return MODE_ESTIMATED_COST.get(mode, MODE_ESTIMATED_COST["balanced"])

    46	def calculate_cost(prompt_tokens: int, completion_tokens: int, model: str, mode: str | None = None) -> int:
    47	    """Calculate credit cost for token usage, with optional mode multiplier."""
    48	    input_rate, output_rate = CREDIT_RATES.get(model, DEFAULT_RATE)
    49	    input_cost = round(prompt_tokens * input_rate / 1000)
    50	    output_cost = round(completion_tokens * output_rate / 1000)
    51	    base_cost = max(1, input_cost + output_cost)
    52	    # Apply mode multiplier
    53	    multiplier = settings.MODE_CREDIT_MULTIPLIER.get(mode or "balanced", 1.0)
    54	    return max(1, int(base_cost * multiplier))

    63	async def debit_credits(
    64	    db: AsyncSession,
    65	    user_id: UUID,
    66	    cost: int,
    67	    reason: str,
    68	    ref_type: Optional[str] = None,
    69	    ref_id: Optional[str] = None,
    70	) -> Optional[UUID]:
    71	    """Atomically debit credits. Returns the CreditLedger entry ID on success, None if insufficient.
    72	
    73	    The balance update and ledger entry are written in the same transaction.
    74	    Caller must call db.commit() to persist changes.
    75	    """
    76	    if cost <= 0:
    77	        raise ValueError("Cost must be positive")
    78	
    79	    result = await db.execute(
    80	        sa.update(User)
    81	        .where(User.id == user_id)
    82	        .where(User.credits_balance >= cost)
    83	        .values(credits_balance=User.credits_balance - cost)
    84	        .returning(User.credits_balance)
    85	    )
    86	    row = result.fetchone()
    87	
    88	    if row is None:
    89	        return None
    90	
    91	    new_balance = row[0]
    92	    ledger = CreditLedger(
    93	        user_id=user_id,
    94	        delta=-cost,
    95	        balance_after=new_balance,
    96	        reason=reason,
    97	        ref_type=ref_type,
    98	        ref_id=ref_id,
    99	    )
   100	    db.add(ledger)
   101	    # Flush to ensure ledger is written in same transaction as balance update
   102	    await db.flush()
   103	    return ledger.id

   106	async def credit_credits(
   107	    db: AsyncSession,
   108	    user_id: UUID,
   109	    amount: int,
   110	    reason: str,

   115	
   116	    The balance update and ledger entry are written in the same transaction.
   117	    Caller must call db.commit() to persist changes.
   118	    """
   119	    if amount <= 0:
   120	        raise ValueError("Amount must be positive")
   121	
   122	    result = await db.execute(
   123	        sa.update(User)

   138	    db.add(ledger)
   139	    # Flush to ensure ledger is written in same transaction as balance update

   195	    reconciliation against a concurrent settlement resolver's conditional

   197	    FIX3-A(c)): whichever of the two transactions gets here first blocks
   198	    the other until it commits or rolls back, so there is no window where
   199	    a resolver can read "not yet reconciled" and a landed commit
   200	    simultaneously. A one-shot existence check (e.g. "does the Message row

   239	    # Update the original ledger entry to reflect actual cost — reconciled_at
   240	    # is now durably stamped in the SAME statement as the delta/balance_after
   241	    # update, never a separate step that could itself be skipped.

   247	            balance_after=CreditLedger.balance_after + diff,
   248	            reconciled_at=sa.func.now(),
   249	        )
   250	    )
   251	    await db.flush()
   252	    return new_balance

   266	    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

   268	    if last is not None and last.tzinfo is None:
   269	        # Treat naive as UTC
   270	        last = last.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)

   659	def _source_location_contract() -> str:
   660	    return (
   661	        "\n\n## Source Locations\n"
   662	        "- Each numbered source may include a location line (e.g. \"source: page 350; section: ...\"). "
   663	        "Location lines are metadata, not new evidence.\n"
   664	        "- When you mention a page, slide, sheet, or document part, use ONLY the location line of the "
   665	        "same [n] you are citing. Never invent a location. If a source has no location line, cite [n] "
   666	        "without claiming a page number.\n"
   667	        "- \"summary coverage\" ranges are approximate; never use them for exact quotations — quote only "
   668	        "from sources that show document text.\n"
   669	        "- For \"what is on page N\" requests, answer only from sources whose location matches page N. "
   670	        "If none match, say page N was not found in the indexed text or is outside the document's range.\n"
   671	    )
   672	
   673	
   674	def _output_terminology_contract() -> str:
   675	    return (
   676	        "\n\n## User-Facing Terminology (applies in EVERY response language)\n"
   677	        "Answer as if you are reading the document directly, not a retrieval system. This rule binds in "
   678	        "whatever language you reply in, including translations of these words. Never refer to the "
   679	        "material as \"fragments\", \"chunks\", \"snippets\", \"excerpts\", \"passages\", \"context blocks\", "
   680	        "or their equivalent in your reply language — e.g. Spanish \"fragmento(s)\"/\"extracto(s)\", "
   681	        "French \"extrait(s)\"/\"fragment(s)\", German \"Auszug\"/\"Fragmente\", Italian \"frammento(i)\", "
   682	        "Portuguese \"trecho(s)\", Arabic \"أجزاء\"/\"مقتطفات\", Chinese \"片段\". Instead say, in your reply "
   683	        "language, \"the document\", \"the text\", \"the section\", or a concrete location like \"page 12\". "
   684	        "If coverage is limited, say the equivalent of \"based on the sections I reviewed\" — never "
   685	        "\"based on the fragments/excerpts\".\n"
   686	    )
   687	
   688	
   689	def _compact_section_list(values: list[str], *, limit: int = 36) -> str:
   690	    unique: list[str] = []
   691	    seen: set[str] = set()
   692	    for value in values:
   693	        section = str(value or "").strip()
   694	        if not section or section in seen:
   695	            continue
   696	        seen.add(section)
   697	        unique.append(section)
   698	    if not unique:
   699	        return "(none)"
   700	    shown = unique[:limit]
   701	    suffix = f"; +{len(unique) - limit} more" if len(unique) > limit else ""
   702	    return ", ".join(shown) + suffix
   703	
   704	
   705	def _summary_coverage_contract(retrieved: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> str:
   706	    target: list[str] = []
   707	    model_covered: list[str] = []
   708	    fallback: list[str] = []
   709	    missing: list[str] = []
   710	    for item in retrieved:
   711	        if item.get("retrieval_modality") != "summary":
   712	            continue
   713	        target.extend(str(section) for section in item.get("map_reduce_target_sections") or [])
   714	        model_covered.extend(
   715	            str(section) for section in item.get("map_reduce_model_covered_sections") or []
   716	        )
   717	        fallback.extend(str(section) for section in item.get("map_reduce_fallback_sections") or [])
   718	        missing.extend(str(section) for section in item.get("map_reduce_missing_sections") or [])
   719	    if not target and not fallback and not missing:
   720	        return ""
   721	    return (
   722	        "\n\n## Summary Coverage Status\n"
   723	        f"- Target sections: {_compact_section_list(target)}\n"
   724	        f"- Model-covered sections: {_compact_section_list(model_covered)}\n"
   725	        f"- Deterministic fallback sections: {_compact_section_list(fallback)}\n"
   726	        f"- Missing sections: {_compact_section_list(missing)}\n"
   727	        "- Treat deterministic fallback sections as lower-confidence source-text coverage. "
   728	        "If fallback or missing sections matter to the answer, say that coverage was limited there.\n"
   729	    )
   730	
   731	
   732	def _retrieval_quality_contract(evaluation: Any | None, strategy: str) -> str:
   733	    if evaluation is None:
   734	        return ""
   735	
   736	    missing_line = (
   737	        f"- Missing evidence-bearing query term count: {len(evaluation.missing_terms)}\n"
   738	        if evaluation.missing_terms
   739	        else ""
   740	    )
   741	    return (
   742	        "\n\n## Retrieval Quality\n"
   743	        f"- Retrieval strategy: {strategy}\n"
   744	        f"- Evidence status: {evaluation.status} ({evaluation.reason})\n"
   745	        f"- Guidance: {evaluation.prompt_note}\n"
   746	        f"{missing_line}"
   747	    )
   748	
   749	
   750	def _query_plan_contract(plan: QueryPlan | None) -> str:
   751	    if plan is None or not plan.is_active:
   752	        return ""
   753	    purposes = sorted({step.purpose for step in plan.steps})
   754	    purpose_text = ", ".join(purposes) if purposes else "direct-answer"
   755	    balanced = (
   756	        "- Balanced per-document coverage was requested for this comparison.\n"
   757	        if plan.needs_balanced_coverage
   758	        else ""
   759	    )
   760	    return (
   761	        "\n\n## Query Plan\n"
   762	        f"- Retrieval was decomposed into {len(plan.steps)} controlled evidence step(s): {purpose_text}.\n"
   763	        f"{balanced}"
   764	        "- For comparison or multi-hop questions, cover each supported side before synthesizing.\n"
   765	        "- If one side has evidence and another side does not, state that asymmetry with citations instead of filling the gap.\n"
   766	    )

   776	async def _refund_predebit(
   777	    db: AsyncSession,
   778	    user_id: uuid.UUID,
   779	    pre_debited: int,
   780	    predebit_ledger_id: uuid.UUID,
   781	) -> bool:
   782	    """Idempotent, RACE-FREE refund for chat failures before final
   783	    accounting.
   784	
   785	    FIX3-A(c) (Codex r3 #4, NOT ADDRESSED): the delete is conditional on
   786	    reconciled_at IS NULL — a SINGLE atomic statement that both checks and
   787	    acts, closing the "read as absent, then a landing commit reconciles,
   788	    then this deletes the now-reconciled row anyway" race a one-shot
   789	    existence read (or a plain unconditional delete) cannot close. If a
   790	    concurrent reconcile_credits() call holds the row's lock (its own
   791	    SELECT ... FOR UPDATE, FIX3-A(b)), this DELETE blocks until that
   792	    transaction resolves, then evaluates the predicate against the
   793	    POST-resolution row state — so it never refunds a row that just got
   794	    reconciled, and never leaves a genuinely unreconciled row un-refunded.
   795	    No read-then-act anywhere.
   796	
   797	    Returns True if a refund was actually issued (the row was still
   798	    unreconciled), False if it was already reconciled or already removed
   799	    by a prior settlement — callers use this to distinguish "nothing was
   800	    delivered" from "this had already landed" without a separate read.
   801	    """
   802	    try:
   803	        await db.rollback()
   804	    except Exception:
   805	        pass
   806	
   807	    result = await db.execute(
   808	        sa.delete(CreditLedger)
   809	        .where(CreditLedger.id == predebit_ledger_id)
   810	        .where(CreditLedger.reconciled_at.is_(None))
   811	        .returning(CreditLedger.id)
   812	    )
   813	    refunded = result.scalar_one_or_none() is not None
   814	    if refunded:
   815	        await db.execute(
   816	            sa.update(User).where(User.id == user_id)
   817	            .values(credits_balance=User.credits_balance + pre_debited)
   818	        )
   819	    else:
   820	        logger.info(
   821	            "quote_billing.already_settled: ledger %s not refunded (already "
   822	            "reconciled or previously removed)", predebit_ledger_id,
   823	        )
   824	    await db.commit()
   825	    return refunded

   831	# client disconnect could pin a task on asyncpg's 60s default connect timeout.

    82	async def save_quote(
    83	    db: AsyncSession, *, user: User, document: Document, card: QuoteCard,
    84	) -> tuple[SavedQuote, bool]:
    85	    """Idempotent insert — returns (row, created=False on an idempotent hit).
    86	
    87	    `card` MUST already be the output of a server-side re-verification
    88	    (quote_search_service.verify_saved_quote() or quote_search()'s own
    89	    cards) — every persisted trust column is copied from it as-is, never
    90	    recomputed here and never sourced from raw request data.
    91	
    92	    SELECT-then-INSERT + IntegrityError-retry, same race biblio_service's
    93	    FIX-9 precedent handles: two concurrent identical saves (double-click,
    94	    two tabs) can both SELECT None then both attempt to INSERT — UNIQUE
    95	    (user_id, document_id, quote_hash) stops the loser's commit, and the
    96	    loser recovers by re-fetching the winner's row instead of surfacing a
    97	    raw 500/409.
    98	
    99	    UNLIKE biblio_service's byte-for-byte pattern, the retry re-fetch below
   100	    runs on a FRESH AsyncSessionLocal() session, not the just-rolled-back
   101	    `db` — discovered via real-Postgres asyncio.gather concurrency testing
   102	    (test_saved_quotes_integration.py) that reusing a NullPool-backed
   103	    session for a new query immediately after a rollback races with
   104	    SQLAlchemy's async/greenlet connection-checkout machinery under true
   105	    concurrent load (reproduced standalone outside pytest too; a
   106	    production QueuePool-backed engine did not exhibit this — NullPool is
   107	    what TESTING=1 forces, per app/models/database.py). A fresh session
   108	    sidesteps it unconditionally and matches the independent-session-for-
   109	    post-failure-resolution pattern already used by the billing resolvers
   110	    in chat_service.py / quotes.py.
   111	
   112	    The cap check (count_active_saved_quotes vs saved_quotes_limit_for_plan)
   113	    is the CALLER's responsibility, performed BEFORE this function runs —
   114	    a documented, accepted narrow TOCTOU window exists between that check
   115	    and this insert (two concurrent NEW saves could both pass a
   116	    same-instant cap check), same class of minor race as the existing
   117	    MAX_DOCUMENTS/share-link caps elsewhere in this codebase; low value,
   118	    self-correcting on the next delete, and not given the money-grade
   119	    locking billing races get.
   120	    """
   121	    quote_hash = compute_quote_hash(card.display_text, card.page, card.page_end)
   122	    existing = await get_existing_saved_quote(
   123	        db, user_id=user.id, document_id=document.id, quote_hash=quote_hash
   124	    )
   125	    if existing is not None:
   126	        return existing, False
   127	
   128	    row = SavedQuote(
   129	        user_id=user.id,
   130	        document_id=document.id,
   131	        page=card.page,
   132	        page_end=card.page_end,
   133	        quote_text=card.display_text,
   134	        bboxes=card.bboxes,
   135	        verification_tier=card.tier,
   136	        verification_score=card.score,
   137	        verifier_version=QUOTE_VERIFIER_VERSION,
   138	        source_chunk_id=uuid.UUID(card.chunk_id),
   139	        source_kind=card.source_kind,
   140	        # M3 review addition (plan §8.1 anchor fields, 2026-08-03): copied
   141	        # from the card as-is, same as every other trust field — None
   142	        # whenever the caller's card didn't come from verify_saved_quote()
   143	        # (the column is nullable for exactly that reason).
   144	        source_text_hash=card.source_text_hash,
   145	        quote_start=card.quote_start,
   146	        quote_end=card.quote_end,
   147	        quote_hash=quote_hash,
   148	        note=None,
   149	    )
   150	    db.add(row)
   151	    try:
   152	        await db.commit()
   153	    except IntegrityError:
   154	        await db.rollback()
   155	        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as retry_db:
   156	            winner = await get_existing_saved_quote(
   157	                retry_db, user_id=user.id, document_id=document.id, quote_hash=quote_hash
   158	            )
   159	        if winner is None:
   160	            raise  # not the race we anticipated — a genuine failure
   161	        return winner, False
   162	    return row, True

    24	async def get_current_user_optional(
    25	    request: Request,
    26	    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db_session),
    27	) -> Optional[User]:
    28	    """Extract user from JWT if present. Returns None for guests."""
    29	    auth_header = request.headers.get("Authorization")
    30	    if not auth_header or not auth_header.startswith("Bearer "):
    31	        return None
    32	
    33	    token = auth_header.split(" ")[1]
    34	    try:
    35	        # Validate AUTH_SECRET is configured
    36	        if not settings.AUTH_SECRET:
    37	            logger.error("AUTH_SECRET not configured")
    38	            return None
    39	
    40	        payload = jwt.decode(
    41	            token,
    42	            settings.AUTH_SECRET,
    43	            algorithms=["HS256"],
    44	            options={
    45	                "verify_aud": False,
    46	                "verify_exp": True,  # Explicitly verify expiration
    47	                "require_exp": True,  # Require exp claim
    48	                "require_iat": True,  # Require issued-at claim
    49	                "require_sub": True,  # Require subject claim
    50	            },
    51	        )
    52	        user_id = payload.get("sub")
    53	        if not user_id:
    54	            return None
    55	        return await db.get(User, UUID(user_id))
    56	    except ExpiredSignatureError:
    57	        log_security_event("auth_failure", reason="token_expired")
    58	        return None
    59	    except JWTClaimsError as e:
    60	        log_security_event("auth_failure", reason="claims_error", detail=str(e))
    61	        return None
    62	    except JWTError as e:
    63	        log_security_event("auth_failure", reason="decode_error", detail=str(e))
    64	        return None
    65

    71	    if not user:

    76	async def require_admin(

    80	    admin_emails = [e.strip() for e in settings.ADMIN_EMAILS.split(",") if e.strip()]
