    98	async def _refund_predebit(db: AsyncSession, user_id: uuid.UUID, pre_debited: int, ledger_id: uuid.UUID) -> bool:
    99	    """Same idempotent, RACE-FREE refund idea as chat_service._refund_predebit,
   100	    NOT a byte-for-byte mirror: that version does its own
   101	    `try: await db.rollback() except: pass` internally before the delete.
   102	    This one does not — callers roll back their OWN session themselves
   103	    first when needed (MINOR-4, review round 1 correction).
   104	
   105	    FIX3-A(c) (Codex r3 #4, NOT ADDRESSED): the delete is conditional on
   106	    reconciled_at IS NULL — a SINGLE atomic statement that both checks and
   107	    acts. See chat_service._refund_predebit's docstring for the full
   108	    race-closure reasoning (identical here). Returns True if a refund was
   109	    actually issued, False if the row was already reconciled or already
   110	    removed by a prior settlement.
   111	    """
   112	    result = await db.execute(
   113	        sa.delete(CreditLedger)
   114	        .where(CreditLedger.id == ledger_id)
   115	        .where(CreditLedger.reconciled_at.is_(None))
   116	        .returning(CreditLedger.id)
   117	    )
   118	    refunded = result.scalar_one_or_none() is not None
   119	    if refunded:
   120	        await db.execute(
   121	            sa.update(User).where(User.id == user_id)
   122	            .values(credits_balance=User.credits_balance + pre_debited)
   123	        )
   124	    else:
   125	        logger.info(
   126	            "quote_billing.already_settled: ledger %s not refunded (already "
   127	            "reconciled or previously removed)", ledger_id,
   128	        )
   129	    await db.commit()
   130	    return refunded

   471	def _verify_against_segments(
   472	    quote_text: str, source: QuoteSource, document: Document,
   473	) -> tuple[list[tuple[Any, QuoteSourceSegment]], Any]:
   474	    """FIX-2 (Codex r1 BLOCKER #2): verify against EACH segment separately —
   475	    never a concatenated multi-page/multi-chunk blob.
   476	
   477	    Returns (matches, best_failure).
   478	
   479	    FIX2-A(b) (Codex r2 #2, NOT ADDRESSED): `matches` holds EVERY verifying
   480	    segment for kind="page_text" — the prior "first segment wins" behavior
   481	    silently dropped genuine duplicate occurrences of the SAME exact wording
   482	    appearing on more than one page within the cited chunk's own page range
   483	    (e.g. a boilerplate clause repeated verbatim). Each is independently
   484	    verified and gets its own card (§8.1 dedup already distinguishes them by
   485	    page, so real duplicates never collapse into one and never over-count).
   486	
   487	    For kind="extracted_text", `matches` holds AT MOST ONE entry — the FIRST
   488	    segment that verifies (segments are ordered cited-chunk-then-neighbors,
   489	    so the cited chunk is always tried before a neighbor). This preserves
   490	    the existing, deliberate simplification that chunk overlap meaning the
   491	    SAME occurrence located via two different chunks collapses to one card
   492	    (see `_dedup_signature`'s docstring) — extracted_text does NOT get the
   493	    multi-match treatment page_text does.
   494	
   495	    `best_failure` is the highest-scoring verify_quote() failure across ALL
   496	    segments tried, for a discard reason when `matches` is empty.
   497	    """
   498	    matches: list[tuple[Any, QuoteSourceSegment]] = []
   499	    best_failure: Any = None
   500	    for segment in source.segments:
   501	        v = verify_quote(
   502	            quote_text, segment.text,
   503	            text_quality=document.text_quality, parse_method=document.parse_method,
   504	        )
   505	        if v.verified:
   506	            matches.append((v, segment))
   507	            if source.kind != "page_text":
   508	                break
   509	            continue
   510	        if best_failure is None or v.score > best_failure.score:
   511	            best_failure = v
   512	    return matches, best_failure

   515	async def quote_search(
   516	    db: AsyncSession,
   517	    *,
   518	    document: Document,
   519	    user: Optional[User],
   520	    topic: str,
   521	    locale: str,
   522	) -> QuoteSearchResult:

   533	
   534	    cards: list[QuoteCard] = []
   535	    discarded: list[tuple[str, str, float]] = []
   536	    seen_keys: set[tuple[str, str, int, int, str]] = set()
   537	
   538	    for item in raw_quotes:
   539	        if not isinstance(item, dict):
   540	            discarded.append(("invalid_proposal", "n/a", 0.0))

   542	
   543	        quote_text = str(item.get("quote_text") or "").strip()
   544	        try:
   545	            ref_n = int(item.get("source_ref_n"))
   546	        except (TypeError, ValueError):
   547	            discarded.append(("ref_out_of_range", "n/a", 0.0))
   548	            continue
   549	        if not quote_text or not (1 <= ref_n <= len(candidates)):
   550	            discarded.append(("ref_out_of_range", "n/a", 0.0))
   551	            continue
   552	
   553	        chunk = candidates[ref_n - 1]
   554	        neighbors = await _neighbor_chunks(db, chunk)
   555	        source: QuoteSource = await build_quote_source(db, document.id, chunk, neighbors)
   556	        matches, best_failure = _verify_against_segments(quote_text, source, document)
   557	
   558	        if not matches:
   559	            if best_failure is None:
   560	                discarded.append(("empty", "dropped", 0.0))
   561	            else:
   562	                reason = best_failure.reason or "not_located"
   563	                discarded.append((reason, best_failure.status, best_failure.score))
   564	            continue

   578	            if key in seen_keys:

   580	            seen_keys.add(key)
   581	
   582	            cards.append(
   583	                QuoteCard(

   585	                    page=page,
   586	                    page_end=page_end,
   587	                    bboxes=bboxes,
   588	                    tier=verification.status,
   589	                    source_kind=source.kind,
   590	                    chunk_id=attributed_chunk_id,
   591	                    score=verification.score,
   592	                )
   593	            )
   594	
   595	    return QuoteSearchResult(

   597	        proposed=len(raw_quotes),
   598	        verified=len(cards),
   599	        discarded=discarded,

   603	        retrieved_count=len(candidates),

   605	        no_result=len(cards) == 0,

   622	    card attributed to a real document, undermining the "no unverified text
   623	    reaches a rendered card" guarantee this system enforces everywhere else
   624	    (M1's verify_quote gate, M2's per-segment attribution). The save
   625	    endpoint therefore supplies ONLY what identifies WHAT to save (chunk_id,
   626	    quote_text); every trust field below is re-derived here through the
   627	    SAME verify_quote gate quote_search() uses, never taken from the caller.
   628	
   629	    `page_hint` disambiguates the rare case where the SAME exact wording
   630	    verifies on more than one page within a page_text chunk's range (a
   631	    repeated boilerplate clause — see _verify_against_segments's
   632	    docstring) — it only picks WHICH already-independently-verified
   633	    occurrence to persist, never fabricates one; an absent or non-matching
   634	    hint just falls back to the first verified occurrence.
   635	
   636	    Returns None when quote_text cannot be independently re-verified
   637	    against the chunk (chunk missing/reparsed since the original search,
   638	    belongs to a different document, or genuinely not a verbatim/
   639	    normalized/aligned match) — callers must reject the save.

   643	        return None
   644	
   645	    neighbors = await _neighbor_chunks(db, chunk)
   646	    source = await build_quote_source(db, document.id, chunk, neighbors)
   647	    matches, _best_failure = _verify_against_segments(quote_text, source, document)
   648	
   649	    # `segment` is carried through alongside the attribution tuple so its
   650	    # own corpus text (the exact string verify_quote checked `quote_text`
   651	    # against) can be hashed below — the "verification corpus" §8.1 wants
   652	    # source_text_hash to represent (page text for page_text segments,
   653	    # chunk text for extracted_text segments).
   654	    attributed = [
   655	        (verification, segment, *_attribute_match(chunk, segment))
   656	        for verification, segment in matches
   657	        if not _is_ambiguous_multipage_extracted_segment(segment)
   658	    ]
   659	    if not attributed:
   660	        return None
   661	
   662	    verification, segment, page, page_end, bboxes, attributed_chunk_id = attributed[0]
   663	    if page_hint is not None:
   664	        for candidate in attributed:
   665	            if candidate[2] == page_hint:  # candidate = (verification, segment, page, ...)
   666	                verification, segment, page, page_end, bboxes, attributed_chunk_id = candidate
   667	                break
   668	
   669	    return QuoteCard(
   670	        display_text=verification.display_text or "",
   671	        page=page,
   672	        page_end=page_end,
   673	        bboxes=bboxes,
   674	        tier=verification.status,
   675	        source_kind=source.kind,
   676	        chunk_id=attributed_chunk_id,
   677	        score=verification.score,
   678	        # M3 review addition (plan §8.1 anchor fields): raw_start/raw_end
   679	        # are the verified slice's exact offsets WITHIN segment.text (see
   680	        # quote_verification_service.verify_quote — every verified tier
   681	        # returns them); source_text_hash lets a future revalidation pass
   682	        # detect whether that corpus has since changed.
   683	        source_text_hash=hashlib.sha256((segment.text or "").encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(),
   684	        quote_start=verification.raw_start,
   685	        quote_end=verification.raw_end,
   686	    )

   247	async def update_note(db: AsyncSession, *, row: SavedQuote, note: Optional[str]) -> SavedQuote:
   248	    """Live E2E bug (team lead, 2026-08-03): PATCH .../quotes/{id} 500'd
   249	    with `sqlalchemy.exc.MissingGreenlet`. Root cause: `updated_at`
   250	    (server-side `onupdate=sa.func.now()`) gets marked EXPIRED by
   251	    SQLAlchemy after this UPDATE's flush — UNLIKE a fresh INSERT (see
   252	    save_quote() above), where INSERT...RETURNING auto-populates
   253	    server_default columns synchronously as part of the flush, an UPDATE's
   254	    onupdate-computed value is NOT auto-refreshed the same way. The
   255	    caller's later SYNCHRONOUS read of that expired attribute
   256	    (`row.updated_at.isoformat()` inside quotes.py's _saved_quote_response,
   257	    which runs as plain Pydantic-model construction, never inside an
   258	    `await`) triggered an implicit lazy DB reload from OUTSIDE an active
   259	    greenlet/await context — exactly MissingGreenlet. `db.refresh(row)`
   260	    here, INSIDE this awaited function, forces that reload to happen
   261	    safely now, so every attribute is a normal in-memory value by the time
   262	    any caller touches it synchronously. Mocked unit tests (row as a bare
   263	    SimpleNamespace) cannot exercise this at all — only a REAL SQLAlchemy
   264	    ORM object has attribute-expiration machinery to trigger it; see
   265	    test_saved_quotes_integration.py's TestSavedQuotesEndpointsRealAsgiRealDb
   266	    for the real-app/real-DB regression coverage."""
   267	    row.note = note
   268	    await db.commit()
   269	    await db.refresh(row)
   270	    return row

The backend cap fix is structurally sound: the lock precedes idempotency/count/insert, every normal exit ends the transaction, and rollback releases it before the fresh-session retry. I found no lock-order cycle with credit reconciliation. Two frontend fixes remain incomplete, so I’m validating their exact failure traces and final line references now.
The eight-commit delta and all seven mapped fixes have been inspected at 81b2725. The lock/cap path is sound; two frontend race/identity fixes remain incomplete.

## Overall verdict: REVISE

At review target `81b2725`: five findings are addressed; #3 and #4 remain incomplete.

### Per-finding verdicts

1. **ADDRESSED — concurrent cap bypass.**  
   The advisory lock is the first statement in `save_quote`; idempotency, cap count, and insert all execute before its transaction ends: [saved_quotes_service.py:145](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/services/saved_quotes_service.py:145), [saved_quotes_service.py:150](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/services/saved_quotes_service.py:150), [saved_quotes_service.py:158](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/services/saved_quotes_service.py:158), [saved_quotes_service.py:164](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/services/saved_quotes_service.py:164). The API no longer performs an unlocked pre-check: [quotes.py:563](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/api/quotes.py:563). The real-HTTP 29/30 distinct-save scenario is correctly covered at [test_saved_quotes_integration.py:329](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/tests/test_saved_quotes_integration.py:329).

2. **ADDRESSED — GDPR export omission.**  
   Saved quotes, notes, and document filenames are queried and included in the export payload: [users.py:334](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/api/users.py:334), [users.py:345](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/api/users.py:345), [users.py:360](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/api/users.py:360).

3. **NOT ADDRESSED — overlapping note saves.**  
   The confirmed baseline and parent propagation fix sequential saves: [SavedQuoteCard.tsx:34](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/frontend/src/components/Quotes/SavedQuoteCard.tsx:34), [SavedQuoteCard.tsx:82](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/frontend/src/components/Quotes/SavedQuoteCard.tsx:82), [QuoteFinderPanel.tsx:170](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/frontend/src/components/Quotes/QuoteFinderPanel.tsx:170). However, every blur still immediately dispatches another PATCH, and the generation guard runs only after that request has completed server-side: [SavedQuoteCard.tsx:73](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/frontend/src/components/Quotes/SavedQuoteCard.tsx:73), [SavedQuoteCard.tsx:80](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/frontend/src/components/Quotes/SavedQuoteCard.tsx:80). The backend remains unconditional last-commit-wins: [saved_quotes_service.py:267](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/services/saved_quotes_service.py:267).

   Therefore, newer PATCH B can commit first, older PATCH A can commit afterward, and the client merely ignores A’s response while the database retains A. Serialize PATCHes or add server-side optimistic concurrency/versioning.

4. **NOT ADDRESSED — card identity key.**  
   The key includes only the first 40 characters of `displayText`: [QuoteCardList.tsx:108](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/frontend/src/components/Quotes/QuoteCardList.tsx:108). Two different quotes sharing the same first 40 characters, chunk, page, and index still reuse the component, preserving `saved=true`; the save guard then blocks the new quote: [QuoteResultCard.tsx:43](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/frontend/src/components/Quotes/QuoteResultCard.tsx:43). Use the full text or a collision-resistant identity and include `pageEnd`.

5. **ADDRESSED — missing FK index.**  
   Migration 0038 creates the requested partial index: [20260803_0038_add_saved_quotes_source_chunk_index.py:36](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/alembic/versions/20260803_0038_add_saved_quotes_source_chunk_index.py:36). ORM metadata matches it: [tables.py:848](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/models/tables.py:848).

6. **ADDRESSED — stale rows after failed refresh.**  
   Rows and count are cleared before fetching: [QuoteFinderPanel.tsx:123](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/frontend/src/components/Quotes/QuoteFinderPanel.tsx:123). The renderer cannot show rows when the request fails: [QuoteFinderPanel.tsx:361](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/frontend/src/components/Quotes/QuoteFinderPanel.tsx:361).

7. **ADDRESSED — incomplete document-name resolution.**  
   The board response now carries `document_filename`: [quotes.py:462](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/api/quotes.py:462), populated after eager-loading the relationship: [saved_quotes_service.py:213](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/services/saved_quotes_service.py:213). The frontend maps and consumes it without `getMyDocuments`: [api.ts:383](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/frontend/src/lib/api.ts:383), [SavedQuotesSection.tsx:139](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/frontend/src/components/Profile/SavedQuotesSection.tsx:139).

### Advisory-lock probe

- **Idempotent save:** Correct. The second request blocks, acquires the lock after the first commit, observes the existing row, skips the cap, commits to release the lock, and returns 200.
- **IntegrityError retry:** Correct for cap safety. `rollback()` releases the transaction-scoped advisory lock before opening the fresh session: [saved_quotes_service.py:187](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/services/saved_quotes_service.py:187). The retry only returns an existing winner and cannot consume another slot.
- **Deadlock surface:** No current lock-order cycle. `save_quote` never locks a credit-ledger row; `reconcile_credits` locks the ledger and may update the user, but never acquires the saved-quote advisory lock: [credit_service.py:204](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/services/credit_service.py:204). At worst there is brief row/FK contention, not a cycle.
- **Coverage caveat:** The identical-save integration test still permits one concurrent request to throw: [test_saved_quotes_integration.py:95](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/tests/test_saved_quotes_integration.py:95), [test_saved_quotes_integration.py:127](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/tests/test_saved_quotes_integration.py:127). With serialization now present, it should require both requests to succeed and return the same row. This is a test-strength gap, not evidence of a runtime defect.

### New breakage in the eight commits

No additional functional regression found beyond incomplete fixes #3 and #4.

Documentation-only drift: [api.ts:433](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/frontend/src/lib/api.ts:433) still says board rows lack filenames and must be joined with `getMyDocuments`. Also, backend comments call `selectinload` a join/no second DB round trip; it actually performs a bounded second SELECT, though still within one HTTP request.

## Overall verdict: REVISE

At review target `81b2725`: five findings are addressed; #3 and #4 remain incomplete.

### Per-finding verdicts

1. **ADDRESSED — concurrent cap bypass.**  
   The advisory lock is the first statement in `save_quote`; idempotency, cap count, and insert all execute before its transaction ends: [saved_quotes_service.py:145](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/services/saved_quotes_service.py:145), [saved_quotes_service.py:150](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/services/saved_quotes_service.py:150), [saved_quotes_service.py:158](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/services/saved_quotes_service.py:158), [saved_quotes_service.py:164](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/services/saved_quotes_service.py:164). The API no longer performs an unlocked pre-check: [quotes.py:563](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/api/quotes.py:563). The real-HTTP 29/30 distinct-save scenario is correctly covered at [test_saved_quotes_integration.py:329](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/tests/test_saved_quotes_integration.py:329).

2. **ADDRESSED — GDPR export omission.**  
   Saved quotes, notes, and document filenames are queried and included in the export payload: [users.py:334](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/api/users.py:334), [users.py:345](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/api/users.py:345), [users.py:360](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/api/users.py:360).

3. **NOT ADDRESSED — overlapping note saves.**  
   The confirmed baseline and parent propagation fix sequential saves: [SavedQuoteCard.tsx:34](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/frontend/src/components/Quotes/SavedQuoteCard.tsx:34), [SavedQuoteCard.tsx:82](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/frontend/src/components/Quotes/SavedQuoteCard.tsx:82), [QuoteFinderPanel.tsx:170](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/frontend/src/components/Quotes/QuoteFinderPanel.tsx:170). However, every blur still immediately dispatches another PATCH, and the generation guard runs only after that request has completed server-side: [SavedQuoteCard.tsx:73](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/frontend/src/components/Quotes/SavedQuoteCard.tsx:73), [SavedQuoteCard.tsx:80](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/frontend/src/components/Quotes/SavedQuoteCard.tsx:80). The backend remains unconditional last-commit-wins: [saved_quotes_service.py:267](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/services/saved_quotes_service.py:267).

   Therefore, newer PATCH B can commit first, older PATCH A can commit afterward, and the client merely ignores A’s response while the database retains A. Serialize PATCHes or add server-side optimistic concurrency/versioning.

4. **NOT ADDRESSED — card identity key.**  
   The key includes only the first 40 characters of `displayText`: [QuoteCardList.tsx:108](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/frontend/src/components/Quotes/QuoteCardList.tsx:108). Two different quotes sharing the same first 40 characters, chunk, page, and index still reuse the component, preserving `saved=true`; the save guard then blocks the new quote: [QuoteResultCard.tsx:43](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/frontend/src/components/Quotes/QuoteResultCard.tsx:43). Use the full text or a collision-resistant identity and include `pageEnd`.

5. **ADDRESSED — missing FK index.**  
   Migration 0038 creates the requested partial index: [20260803_0038_add_saved_quotes_source_chunk_index.py:36](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/alembic/versions/20260803_0038_add_saved_quotes_source_chunk_index.py:36). ORM metadata matches it: [tables.py:848](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/models/tables.py:848).

6. **ADDRESSED — stale rows after failed refresh.**  
   Rows and count are cleared before fetching: [QuoteFinderPanel.tsx:123](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/frontend/src/components/Quotes/QuoteFinderPanel.tsx:123). The renderer cannot show rows when the request fails: [QuoteFinderPanel.tsx:361](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/frontend/src/components/Quotes/QuoteFinderPanel.tsx:361).
